# MODEL LIST — every 3D model in Entropy Wars and how it was made

GENERATED by `node list-models.js` from the game's own tables — do not hand-edit this file (the hand-kept parts are in
`MODEL_LIST_EXTRA.md`, copied in below). Last written 2026-11-03.

**THE RULE (mondo, 2026-11-03):** every model added to the game — a character, a spell prop, a room prop, a pack, a
creator piece — is documented here in the SAME PR that wires it: add a `SOURCES` row in list-models.js if it comes from
a new folder / pack / maker, rerun `node list-models.js`, commit MODEL_LIST.md. Name the pack and its author when known.
Goal: keep the look consistent and track how far the game is from **all human-made models** (see the tally).
Where each model STANDS (which map, room, spell) is MODEL_INDEX.md; this file is WHAT exists and WHO / WHAT made it.

## 1. The tally

| made by | human-made? | count |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Asset pack | yes | 156 |
| Asset pack (author not recorded yet) | yes | 166 |
| MakeHuman community asset | yes | 546 |
| MakeHuman parts, baked by Claude in Blender | yes (parts) | 20 |
| mondo's own model | yes | 1 |
| mondo's own model, texture by Meshy AI (Retexture on his UVs) | yes (mesh) | 2 |
| Meshy AI | no | 607 |
| Meshy AI, reworked by Claude | no | 7 |
| Claude in Blender | no | 105 |
| Claude in code (three.js, no file) | no | 93 |

Counts are distinct files (a pack kit cut into pieces counts each piece used; the MakeHuman count is the creator's
picks, each a GLB per body; the code count is procedural catalogue rows).
"Claude in code" = procedural stand-ins drawn from three.js primitives — the props still waiting for a real model (§8, ranked in §10).
Not in the count: the horizon. **65 of the 172 builders drawn past the board** (far rosters, near settings, rims, room weenies, board
monuments) are three.js shapes with no model file — §8b lists them, §11 says which look bad and what could replace them.

Every race in the game. "creator look also baked" = a character-creator build of the race exists as a saved option

### The Meshy decor (three-renderer.js _HQ_DECOR_GLB) — worn by the procs in §8 (81)

Made 2026-11-03 for the code-drawn stand-ins of §10A (D-IDs in project files meshy/decor/decor_prompts.json). A proc builder
calls `_hqDecorWear(name, U, g)`: its own pieces hide (they keep the collision) and the model is fitted into their box.

## 8b. The horizon and the sky — everything drawn past the board, and what it is made of

Written by list-models.js from three-renderer.js's own tables (2026-11-03: the cone mountains had no row because this list
only read model files). **made with** = what the scan found in the builder and the helpers it calls: model files (their
maker in brackets, from the tables above) and/or three.js shapes. "three.js shapes (code)" = drawn by Claude in code, no
model: those are the candidates for a real model. Also painted, not modelled: the battle's ring-wall skyline (distant
snow mountains drawn in its shader), the sky dome, stars, clouds and the lenticular (`_hzLenticular`, a cloud sprite stack).

**65 of these 172 rows are code only (three.js shapes / glow sprites, no model file); the monuments reuse many roster builders, so a builder can count twice.**

**The cone mountains are gone** (2026-11-03, mondo: "they look terrible"): the rooms' far weenies `peak` / `mountain`
(Mount Shasta over the woods and the ranch, the Range over Area 51, the Transantarctics, Olympus Mons, the Clash temple's
three) were stacked three.js cones with a white cone for snow. Builders and rows removed, nothing replaces them: no downloaded
pack has a mountain, and the D.O.O.R. kit asteroid rocks stretched into a massif read as giant blobs.
**Wanted:** a real far mountain range model (one file, low poly) if mondo wants the weenies back.

## 10. DECOR STILL MISSING — the props the rooms need that have no real model (2026-11-03)

Built from the 184 code-drawn catalogue procs of 2026-11-03 (§8 now lists what is left (each already placed in a room: "uses" = placements in data.js) and the
old per-zone wishlists in MODEL_INDEX.md §3f-3p / §6b. Every stand-in falls in exactly one tier below.
Meshy process (mondo's): concept image first -> mondo approves -> model in low-poly smart topology -> mondo names redos.
Target tris are doubled per mondo (MPFB bodies ~30k). Concept rules: one object, plain background, no strings / chains / cords, screens and signs blank. Wiring a model into the
game (giving the catalogue row a `file` and dropping its `proc`) is a separate step, only on mondo's say.
**2026-11-03 mondo said wire them:** the 71 models Meshy made are in (rows marked WIRED). Each proc builder now WEARS its model
(three-renderer.js THE MESHY DECOR, `_hqDecorWear`, files on R2 `Assets/decor/`): the catalogue rows are unchanged, the proc's
own pieces hide (and keep the collision) and the model is fitted into their box. Rows not marked were not made on Meshy.
